The app preserves the proposed automatic decision alongside the frozen pending selection. Loading captures the real pre-allocation machine and pack components, authenticates the supported files, applies the exact recipe and obtains Engine's observation after its bounded health check. The durable activation commit precedes publication of any confirmed profile. Confirmation failure leaves the healthy model usable with unknown speed evidence. A restored earlier load explicitly receives no evidence from the failed new selection. Reconfiguration and unload clear the generation binding; user preferences are not rewritten.

Telemetry compares the current complete allocation digest, generation, independent user controls and hardware/storage/thermal/power observation with the confirmed load. Pending settings, activation failure, OS pressure, a smaller allocation or changed conditions withhold the profile label, measured rate and target-met flag. Readiness and an inactive custom value do not invalidate unchanged execution. Polling reads the separately published plan and metadata only; it does not take the generation lock, authenticate files or force tensors to load. Missing evidence is stated plainly, and an unloaded proposal cannot be presented as measured execution.

Prepared synthetic fixtures cover reload/rollback generation, absent or simulated plans, changed allocation under the same saved ceiling, thermal/power/storage changes or unreadable conditions, pressure, independent user controls, and readiness. The fixtures create no model and grant no product profile. The diff check passes; native compilation/execution of this application change and real loaded observation remain pending. The registry remains original-only with no qualified automatic profiles.

The preceding setup-view commit 05d4ec35cd47d7680ca57f53445e157032cb0c0b has a completed successful scripted-check job 111583794375 in run 37252832951, including all twelve setup states across Light, Dark and System, original performance and repair checks, and the no-Metal repeat. That run's Xcode job preserves the already diagnosed package-access failure. The corrective fbb109afb90f4e3c383a8b0ff21f0ac03456fae1 Xcode job 111586873310 succeeded; its separate scripted job was still running at capture. Neither result is attributed to this uncompiled telemetry edit.

Artifact 11321343418 contains the native setup renders. Its receipt below pins the downloaded archive and the twelve extracted PNGs. Manual inspection of light-setup-reviewed, dark-setup-stale, light-setup-verified and system-setup-unavailable found readable text and controls, correct changed-settings and unavailable-choice explanations, and verified-files wording distinct from loaded readiness. The automated checks cover all twelve. These are fixture screenshots, not a completed physical download or activation. The fixture's available-storage number is a deterministic rendering input, not a hardware measurement.

No installed CLI, weight bytes, profile registry or public release changes. The frozen held-out continuation remains active, with no partial answer, score or aggregate inspection. Native quality, product outcomes, performance, standalone packaging and final physical acceptance are still required.
